110+  67C3 C9                   RET

  6+  67D5
  7+  67D5              FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  8+  67D5              IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  9+  67D5              IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
 10+  67D5
 11+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  67D5              ; Open the single NeoGS package on TR-DOS and read its first sector to #C000.
 13+  67D5              ; The same 256-byte buffer is reused for every following sector while the
 14+  67D5              ; firmware is streamed to NeoGS. The complete file is NEVER staged in host RAM.
 15+  67D5              ; OUT: Carry clear, first sector at #C000, file length and next-sector cursor set.
 16+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  67D5              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  67D5
 18+  67D5                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  67D5 21 9C 6B             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  67D8 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
 21+  67DB 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
 22+  67DE ED B0                ldir
 23+  67E0 3E 43                ld      a, 'C'
 24+  67E2 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  67E5 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  67E7 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  67EA 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
 28+  67EC CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 29+  67EF 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  67F0 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  67F2 CA 57 68             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  67F5
 33+  67F5                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  67F5 F5                   push    af
 35+  67F6 AF                   xor     a
 36+  67F7 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  67FA 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  67FD F1                   pop     af
 39+  67FE 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6800 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  6803 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  6806 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  6807 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  680A B0                   or      b
 45+  680B C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  680E
 47+  680E                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  680E 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  6811 22 AC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  6814 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  6815 B5                   or      l
 52+  6816 CA 5F 68             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  6819 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  681A B7                   or      a
 55+  681B 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  681D 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  681E FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6820 DA 5F 68             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  6823              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  6823                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 61+  6823 2A AC 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 62+  6826 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 63+  6829 19                   add     hl, de
 64+  682A 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 65+  682D BC                   cp      h
 66+  682E C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 67+  6831 B7                   or      a
 68+  6832 CA 5B 68             jp      z, .bad_descriptor
 69+  6835 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 70+  6838
 71+  6838 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 72+  683B FE 10                cp      16
 73+  683D D2 5B 68             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 74+  6840 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 75+  6843 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 76+  6846 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 77+  6849 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 78+  684C 22 A4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 79+  684F
 80+  684F                      ; Read only the first sector now. Later sectors are fetched on demand
 81+  684F                      ; by upload_firmware after the preceding buffer bytes have been sent.
 82+  684F CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
 83+  6852 DA 63 68             jp      c, .sector_read_failed
 84+  6855 AF                   xor     a
 85+  6856 C9                   ret
 86+  6857
 87+  6857              .file_not_found:
 88+  6857 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 89+  6859 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
 90+  685B              .bad_descriptor:
 91+  685B 3E 02                ld      a, 2
 92+  685D 18 06                jr      .load_failed
 93+  685F              .bad_length:
 94+  685F 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 95+  6861 18 02                jr      .load_failed
 96+  6863              .sector_read_failed:
 97+  6863 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 98+  6865              .load_failed:
 99+  6865 32 AB 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_error), a
100+  6868 37                   scf
101+  6869 C9                   ret
102+  686A
103+  686A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
104+  686A              ; Read one TR-DOS sector into the reusable #C000 buffer.
105+  686A              ; Uses neo_fw_load_track/sector/remaining. Advances the cursor only after a
106+  686A              ; successful read. OUT: carry clear; carry set on disk error/exhaustion.
107+  686A              ; Clobbers AF, BC, DE, HL.
108+  686A              neo_fw_read_next_sector:
109+  686A 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
110+  686D B7                   or      a
111+  686E 20 02                jr      nz, .sector_available
112+  6870 37                   scf
113+  6871 C9                   ret
114+  6872              .sector_available:
115+  6872 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
116+  6875 57                   ld      d, a
117+  6876 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
118+  6879 5F                   ld      e, a
119+  687A 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
120+  687D 06 01                ld      b, 1
121+  687F 0E 05                ld      c, 5
122+  6881 AF                   xor     a
123+  6882 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
124+  6885 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
125+  6888 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
126+  688B 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
127+  688E 47                   ld      b, a
128+  688F 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
129+  6892 B0                   or      b
130+  6893 20 21                jr      nz, .sector_io_error
131+  6895
132+  6895                      ; Advance TR-DOS cursor: sectors 0..15, then next track.
133+  6895 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
134+  6898 3C                   inc     a
135+  6899 FE 10                cp      16
136+  689B 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
137+  689D AF                   xor     a
138+  689E 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
139+  68A1 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
140+  68A4 3C                   inc     a
141+  68A5 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
142+  68A8 18 03                jr      .sector_cursor_advanced
143+  68AA              .sector_same_track:
144+  68AA 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
145+  68AD              .sector_cursor_advanced:
146+  68AD 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
147+  68B0 3D                   dec     a
148+  68B1 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
149+  68B4 B7                   or      a
150+  68B5 C9                   ret
151+  68B6              .sector_io_error:
152+  68B6 37                   scf
153+  68B7 C9                   ret
154+  68B8
155+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
156+  68B8              ; upload_firmware
157+  68B8              ; IN : HL = #C000 reusable first-sector buffer, DE = total package length.
158+  68B8              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
159+  68B8              ;
160+  68B8              ; Cold/warm reset path:
161+  68B8              ;   #F4 cold restart
162+  68B8              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
163+  68B8              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
164+  68B8              ;   #13 jump to $4000
165+  68B8              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
166+  68B8              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
167+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
168+  68B8              upload_firmware:
168+  68B8
169+  68B8                      ; Only the first sector is resident at #C000. Remaining sectors are
170+  68B8                      ; read into this same buffer as the firmware stream consumes them.
171+  68B8                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
172+  68B8 22 AE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
173+  68BB ED 53 B0 6B          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
174+  68BF
175+  68BF                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
176+  68BF 7A                   ld      a, d
177+  68C0 B7                   or      a
178+  68C1 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
179+  68C3 7B                   ld      a, e
180+  68C4 FE 03                cp      3
181+  68C6 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
182+  68C9              .package_header_present:
183+  68C9 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
184+  68CA 32 B4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
185+  68CD 23                   inc     hl
186+  68CE 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
187+  68CF 32 B5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
188+  68D2 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
189+  68D5 7C                   ld      a, h
190+  68D6 B5                   or      l
191+  68D7 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
192+  68DA                      ; Bootstrap must fit entirely in the first 256-byte sector because it
193+  68DA                      ; is needed before the receiver can be started. The package itself may
194+  68DA                      ; be much larger; only this first sector is buffered at a time.
195+  68DA 7C                   ld      a, h
196+  68DB B7                   or      a
197+  68DC C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
198+  68DF 7D                   ld      a, l
199+  68E0 FE FF                cp      255                   ; maximum length 254 bytes
200+  68E2 D2 83 6A             jp      nc, .upload_failed
201+  68E5
202+  68E5                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
203+  68E5 2A B0 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
204+  68E8 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
205+  68EB B7                   or      a
206+  68EC ED 52                sbc     hl, de
207+  68EE DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
208+  68F1 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
209+  68F5 B7                   or      a
210+  68F6 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
211+  68F8 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
212+  68FB 22 B8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
213+  68FE
214+  68FE                      ; Bootstrap and the beginning of firmware follow the two-byte header
215+  68FE                      ; in the first sector. The firmware pointer is advanced as bytes go.
216+  68FE 2A AE 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
217+  6901 23                   inc     hl
218+  6902 23                   inc     hl
219+  6903 22 B2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
220+  6906 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
221+  690A 19                   add     hl, de
222+  690B 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
223+  690E
224+  690E                      ; Number of firmware bytes remaining in the first sector:
225+  690E                      ; 256 - (2-byte header + bootstrap length) = 254 - bootstrap length.
226+  690E 21 FE 00             ld      hl, 254
227+  6911 B7                   or      a
228+  6912 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
229+  6914 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
230+  6917
231+  6917                      ; Firmware low-window image must fit in $0000-$3FFF; stack is now in reserved WIN3.
232+  6917 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
233+  691A 7C                   ld      a, h
234+  691B B7                   or      a
235+  691C C2 26 69             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
236+  691F 7D                   ld      a, l
237+  6920 B7                   or      a
238+  6921 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
239+  6924 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
240+  6926              .firmware_high_nonzero:
241+  6926 7C                   ld      a, h
242+  6927 FE 40                cp      #40
243+  6929 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
244+  692B C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
245+  692E 7D                   ld      a, l
246+  692F B7                   or      a
247+  6930 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
248+  6933              .firmware_length_valid:
249+  6933 AF                   xor     a
250+  6934 32 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
251+  6937 32 C5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
252+  693A 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
253+  693D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
254+  693F 32 C6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
255+  6942 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
256+  6945
257+  6945                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
258+  6945                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
259+  6945 3E 01                ld      a, 1
260+  6947 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
261+  694A 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
262+  694C CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
263+  694F DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
264+  6952
265+  6952                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
266+  6952                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
267+  6952                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
268+  6952                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
269+  6952 3E 02                ld      a, 2
270+  6954 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
271+  6957 CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
272+  695A 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
273+  695C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
274+  695E 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
275+  6961 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
276+  6963 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
277+  6966 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
278+  6969 3E 02                ld      a, 2
279+  696B 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
280+  696E CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
281+  6971 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
282+  6974              .stock_rom_ready:
283+  6974
284+  6974                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
285+  6974                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
286+  6974                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
287+  6974                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
288+  6974 3E 03                ld      a, 3
289+  6976 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
290+  6979 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
291+  697C ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
292+  6980 B7                   or      a
293+  6981 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
294+  6983 22 BC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
295+  6986 7D                   ld      a, l
296+  6987 CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
297+  698A DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
298+  698D 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
299+  698F CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
300+  6992 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
301+  6995
302+  6995                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
303+  6995 3A BD 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
304+  6998 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
305+  699B DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
306+  699E AF                   xor     a
307+  699F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
308+  69A2 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
309+  69A5 3E 40                ld      a, #40
310+  69A7 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
311+  69AA DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
312+  69AD
313+  69AD                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
314+  69AD 3E 04                ld      a, 4
315+  69AF 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
316+  69B2 2A B2 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
317+  69B5 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
318+  69B9              .bootstrap_block_loop:
319+  69B9 7A                   ld      a, d
320+  69BA B3                   or      e
321+  69BB 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
322+  69BD 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
323+  69BE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
324+  69C1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
325+  69C4 23                   inc     hl
326+  69C5 1B                   dec     de
327+  69C6 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
328+  69C8              .bootstrap_block_done:
329+  69C8
330+  69C8                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
331+  69C8                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
332+  69C8 3E 05                ld      a, 5
333+  69CA 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
334+  69CD AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
335+  69CE CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
336+  69D1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
337+  69D4 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
338+  69D6 CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
339+  69D9 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
340+  69DC 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
341+  69DE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
342+  69E1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
343+  69E4
344+  69E4                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
345+  69E4 3E 06                ld      a, 6
346+  69E6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
347+  69E9 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
348+  69EC DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
349+  69EF FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
350+  69F1 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
351+  69F4
352+  69F4                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
353+  69F4 3E 07                ld      a, 7
354+  69F6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
355+  69F9 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
356+  69FC 7D                   ld      a, l
357+  69FD CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
358+  6A00 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
359+  6A03 7C                   ld      a, h
360+  6A04 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
361+  6A07 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
362+  6A0A CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
363+  6A0D DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
364+  6A10 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
365+  6A12 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
366+  6A15
367+  6A15                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
368+  6A15 3E 08                ld      a, 8
369+  6A17 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
370+  6A1A 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
371+  6A1D ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
372+  6A21              .firmware_stream_loop:
373+  6A21 7A                   ld      a, d
374+  6A22 B3                   or      e
375+  6A23 28 31                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
376+  6A25
377+  6A25                      ; Refill the same 256-byte host buffer only after its current bytes
378+  6A25                      ; have all been consumed. DE (firmware bytes left) survives the read.
379+  6A25 ED 4B A7 6B          ld      bc, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
380+  6A29 78                   ld      a, b
381+  6A2A B1                   or      c
382+  6A2B 20 11                jr      nz, .firmware_buffer_ready
383+  6A2D D5                   push    de
384+  6A2E CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
385+  6A31 D1                   pop     de
386+  6A32 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
387+  6A35 21 00 01             ld      hl, 256
388+  6A38 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
389+  6A3B 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
390+  6A3E              .firmware_buffer_ready:
391+  6A3E 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
392+  6A3F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
393+  6A42 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
394+  6A45 23                   inc     hl
395+  6A46 1B                   dec     de
396+  6A47 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
397+  6A4A 2A A7 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
398+  6A4D 2B                   dec     hl
399+  6A4E 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
400+  6A51 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
401+  6A54 18 CB                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
402+  6A56
403+  6A56              .firmware_stream_done:
404+  6A56                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
405+  6A56 3E 09                ld      a, 9
406+  6A58 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
407+  6A5B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
408+  6A5E DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
409+  6A61 FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
410+  6A63 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
411+  6A66
412+  6A66                      ; Cross-check every GS data byte acknowledged by DBIT. The count
413+  6A66                      ; includes #14's remaining three header bytes, bootstrap payload,
414+  6A66                      ; #13's high address byte, firmware length (two bytes), and firmware.
415+  6A66                      ; The first argument byte of #14 and #13 is preloaded before command
416+  6A66                      ; dispatch and is therefore intentionally not counted.
417+  6A66 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
418+  6A68 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
419+  6A6B 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
420+  6A6E ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
421+  6A72 19                   add     hl, de
422+  6A73 11 06 00             ld      de, 6
423+  6A76 19                   add     hl, de
424+  6A77 ED 5B C4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
425+  6A7B B7                   or      a
426+  6A7C ED 52                sbc     hl, de
427+  6A7E C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
428+  6A81
429+  6A81 AF                   xor     a
430+  6A82 C9                   ret
431+  6A83
432+  6A83              .upload_failed:
433+  6A83 37                   scf
434+  6A84 C9                   ret
435+  6A85
436+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
437+  6A85              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
438+  6A85              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
439+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
440+  6A85
441+  6A85              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
442+  6A85              gs_boot_probe_rom:
443+  6A85 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
444+  6A87 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
445+  6A8A D8                   ret     c
446+  6A8B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
447+  6A8E D8                   ret     c
448+  6A8F 32 BE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
449+  6A92 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
450+  6A95 D8                   ret     c
451+  6A96 32 BF 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
452+  6A99 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
453+  6A9C D8                   ret     c
454+  6A9D 32 C0 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
455+  6AA0                      ; #20 reports total RAM as a 24-bit byte count. The 48-KiB
456+  6AA0                      ; memory map needs at least four 16-KiB pages (64 KiB total):
457+  6AA0                      ; fixed pages 0 and 3 plus selectable pages 1 and 2.
458+  6AA0 3A C0 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
459+  6AA3 B7                   or      a
460+  6AA4 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
461+  6AA6 37                   scf
462+  6AA7 C9                   ret
463+  6AA8              .rom_probe_ok:
464+  6AA8 B7                   or      a
465+  6AA9 C9                   ret
466+  6AAA
467+  6AAA              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
468+  6AAA              gs_boot_send_command_long:
469+  6AAA 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
470+  6AAD 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
471+  6AB0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
472+  6AB2 D5                   push    de
473+  6AB3 16 10                ld      d, 16
474+  6AB5              .long_outer:
475+  6AB5 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
476+  6AB8              .long_inner:
477+  6AB8 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
478+  6ABA 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
479+  6ABD CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
480+  6ABF 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
481+  6AC1 0B                   dec     bc
482+  6AC2 78                   ld      a, b
483+  6AC3 B1                   or      c
484+  6AC4 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
485+  6AC6 15                   dec     d
486+  6AC7 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
487+  6AC9 D1                   pop     de
488+  6ACA 37                   scf
489+  6ACB C9                   ret
490+  6ACC              .long_done:
491+  6ACC D1                   pop     de
492+  6ACD B7                   or      a
493+  6ACE C9                   ret
494+  6ACF
495+  6ACF              gs_boot_send_command:
496+  6ACF 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
497+  6AD2 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
498+  6AD5 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
499+  6AD7 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
500+  6ADA              .command_wait:
501+  6ADA DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
502+  6ADC 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
503+  6ADF CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
504+  6AE1 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
505+  6AE3 0B                   dec     bc
506+  6AE4 78                   ld      a, b
507+  6AE5 B1                   or      c
508+  6AE6 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
509+  6AE8 37                   scf
510+  6AE9 C9                   ret
511+  6AEA              .command_done:
512+  6AEA B7                   or      a
513+  6AEB C9                   ret
514+  6AEC
515+  6AEC              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
516+  6AEC              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
517+  6AEC              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
518+  6AEC 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
519+  6AEF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
520+  6AF2              .argument_wait_free:
521+  6AF2 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
522+  6AF4 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
523+  6AF7 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
524+  6AF9 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
525+  6AFB 0B                   dec     bc
526+  6AFC 78                   ld      a, b
527+  6AFD B1                   or      c
528+  6AFE 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
529+  6B00 37                   scf
530+  6B01 C9                   ret
531+  6B02              .argument_slot_free:
532+  6B02 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
533+  6B05 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
534+  6B07 B7                   or      a
535+  6B08 C9                   ret
536+  6B09
537+  6B09              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
538+  6B09 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
539+  6B0C 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
540+  6B0F              .data_wait_free:
541+  6B0F DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
542+  6B11 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
543+  6B14 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
544+  6B16 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
545+  6B18 0B                   dec     bc
546+  6B19 78                   ld      a, b
547+  6B1A B1                   or      c
548+  6B1B 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
549+  6B1D 37                   scf
550+  6B1E C9                   ret
551+  6B1F              .data_slot_free:
552+  6B1F 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
553+  6B22 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
554+  6B24 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
555+  6B27              .data_wait_consumed:
556+  6B27 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
557+  6B29 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
558+  6B2C CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
559+  6B2E 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
560+  6B30 0B                   dec     bc
561+  6B31 78                   ld      a, b
562+  6B32 B1                   or      c
563+  6B33 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
564+  6B35 37                   scf
565+  6B36 C9                   ret
566+  6B37              .data_consumed:
567+  6B37 E5                   push    hl
568+  6B38 2A C4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
569+  6B3B 23                   inc     hl
570+  6B3C 22 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
571+  6B3F E1                   pop     hl
572+  6B40 B7                   or      a
573+  6B41 C9                   ret
574+  6B42
575+  6B42              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
576+  6B42 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
577+  6B45              .receive_wait:
578+  6B45 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
579+  6B47 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
580+  6B4A CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
581+  6B4C 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
582+  6B4E 0B                   dec     bc
583+  6B4F 78                   ld      a, b
584+  6B50 B1                   or      c
585+  6B51 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
586+  6B53 37                   scf
587+  6B54 C9                   ret
588+  6B55              .receive_ready:

590+  6B57 32 C3 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
591+  6B5A B7                   or      a
592+  6B5B C9                   ret

595+  6B5C              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
596+  6B5C              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6BC9              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6BC9              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6BC9              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6BC9              ;
  6+  6BC9              ; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
  7+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
  9+  6BC9              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6BC9              ;
 11+  6BC9              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 12+  6BC9              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 13+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
 14+  6BC9
 15+  6BC9              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 16+  6BC9
 17+  6BC9              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 18+  6BC9              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 19+  6BC9

 23+  6BCF ED 73 BB 7B          ld      (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP), sp
 24+  6BD3 31 FF BF             ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it

180+  6D64 2A CF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)

182+  6D6A B7                   or      a
183+  6D6B ED 52                sbc     hl, de
184+  6D6D C2 2F 70             jp      nz, .int_guest_fail
185+  6D70                      ; Stage 2B reports each regression group separately. These messages are
186+  6D70                      ; emitted only after the complete guest image and all final state
187+  6D70                      ; assertions have passed.
188+  6D70                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_mov_flags_pass

189+  6D7F E1          >                    POP     HL

199+  6DC8
200+  6DC8                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
201+  6DC8                      ; Stage 3A: guest INT 10h video BIOS services and live console stream.
202+  6DC8                      ; The NeoGS sends each teletype byte over DBIT while this host drains
203+  6DC8                      ; the stream to port #EF and the 42x12 6x8 debug window.
204+  6DC8                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
205+  6DC8                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_start
205+  6DC8             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
205+  6DC8 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
205+  6DC9 21 80 7A    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_bios_video_start
205+  6DCC CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
205+  6DCF E1          >                    POP     HL
205+  6DD0             >                ENDIF
206+  6DD0 CD FF 71             call    neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result
207+  6DD3 DA 68 70             jp      c, .bios_video_transport_fail
208+  6DD6 3A CE 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
209+  6DD9 B7                   or      a
210+  6DDA C2 74 70             jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
211+  6DDD 2A CF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
212+  6DE0 11 10 1A             ld      de, #1A10
213+  6DE3 B7                   or      a
214+  6DE4 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
215+  6DE6 C2 74 70             jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
216+  6DE9                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_pass
216+  6DE9             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
216+  6DE9 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
216+  6DEA 21 C2 7A    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_bios_video_pass
216+  6DED CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
216+  6DF0 E1          >                    POP     HL
216+  6DF1             >                ENDIF
217+  6DF1
218+  6DF1                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
219+  6DF1                      ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
220+  6DF1                      ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
221+  6DF1                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
222+  6DF1 2A C3 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
223+  6DF4 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
224+  6DF7 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
225+  6DFA ED B0                ldir
226+  6DFC 3A C5 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
227+  6DFF 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
228+  6E02 3E 09                ld      a, 9
229+  6E04 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
230+  6E07 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
231+  6E09 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
232+  6E0C 79                   ld      a, c
233+  6E0D FE FF                cp      #FF
234+  6E0F CA 13 71             jp      z, .file_not_found
235+  6E12
236+  6E12                      ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
237+  6E12                      ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
238+  6E12 79                   ld      a, c
239+  6E13 F5                   push    af
240+  6E14 AF                   xor     a
241+  6E15 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
242+  6E18 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
243+  6E1B F1                   pop     af
244+  6E1C 0E 08                ld      c, 8
245+  6E1E CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
246+  6E21 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
247+  6E24 47                   ld      b, a
248+  6E25 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
249+  6E28 B0                   or      b
250+  6E29 C2 2B 71             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
251+  6E2C
252+  6E2C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok
252+  6E2C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
252+  6E2C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
252+  6E2D 21 06 7E    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
252+  6E30 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
252+  6E33 E1          >                    POP     HL
252+  6E34             >                ENDIF
253+  6E34
254+  6E34                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
255+  6E34                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
256+  6E34                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
257+  6E34                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
258+  6E34                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
259+  6E34                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
260+  6E34                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
261+  6E34 ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
262+  6E38 78                   ld      a, b
263+  6E39 B1                   or      c
264+  6E3A CA 1F 71             jp      z, .empty_file_error
265+  6E3D ED 43 C6 7B          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
266+  6E41
267+  6E41                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
268+  6E41 78                   ld      a, b
269+  6E42 FE 2F                cp      #2F
270+  6E44 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
271+  6E46 C2 58 71             jp      nz, .task_too_large
272+  6E49 79                   ld      a, c
273+  6E4A FE FF                cp      #FF
274+  6E4C D2 58 71             jp      nc, .task_too_large
275+  6E4F              .file_length_ok:
276+  6E4F                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
277+  6E4F 2A C6 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
278+  6E52 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
279+  6E55 19                   add     hl, de
280+  6E56 5C                   ld      e, h
281+  6E57 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
282+  6E5A B7                   or      a
283+  6E5B CA 1F 71             jp      z, .empty_file_error
284+  6E5E BB                   cp      e
285+  6E5F C2 2B 71             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
286+  6E62 32 CA 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
287+  6E65
288+  6E65 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
289+  6E68 FE 10                cp      16
290+  6E6A D2 2B 71             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
291+  6E6D 32 CC 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
292+  6E70 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
293+  6E73 32 CB 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
294+  6E76 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
295+  6E79 22 C8 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
296+  6E7C
297+  6E7C              .read_sector_loop:
298+  6E7C                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
299+  6E7C 3A CB 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
300+  6E7F 57                   ld      d, a
301+  6E80 3A CC 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
302+  6E83 5F                   ld      e, a
303+  6E84 2A C8 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
304+  6E87 06 01                ld      b, 1
305+  6E89 0E 05                ld      c, 5
306+  6E8B AF                   xor     a
307+  6E8C 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
308+  6E8F 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
309+  6E92 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
310+  6E95                      ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
311+  6E95 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
312+  6E98 47                   ld      b, a
313+  6E99 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
314+  6E9C B0                   or      b
315+  6E9D C2 37 71             jp      nz, .sector_read_error
316+  6EA0
317+  6EA0                      ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
318+  6EA0 2A C8 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
319+  6EA3 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
320+  6EA6 19                   add     hl, de
321+  6EA7 22 C8 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
322+  6EAA
323+  6EAA                      ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
324+  6EAA 3A CC 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
325+  6EAD 3C                   inc     a
326+  6EAE FE 10                cp      16
327+  6EB0 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
328+  6EB2 AF                   xor     a
329+  6EB3 32 CC 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
330+  6EB6 3A CB 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
331+  6EB9 3C                   inc     a
332+  6EBA 32 CB 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
333+  6EBD 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
334+  6EBF
335+  6EBF              .sector_same_track:
336+  6EBF 32 CC 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
337+  6EC2
338+  6EC2              .sector_advanced:
339+  6EC2 3A CA 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING)
340+  6EC5 3D                   dec     a
341+  6EC6 32 CA 7B             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
342+  6EC9 C2 7C 6E             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
343+  6ECC
344+  6ECC                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_buf_loaded
344+  6ECC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
344+  6ECC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
344+  6ECD 21 2A 7E    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_buf_loaded
344+  6ED0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
344+  6ED3 E1          >                    POP     HL
344+  6ED4             >                ENDIF
345+  6ED4
346+  6ED4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
347+  6ED4                      ; Stage 5C: transfer actual task. The DMA frame contains a two-byte
348+  6ED4                      ; length prefix, therefore the total transfer must fit in $3000 bytes.
349+  6ED4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
350+  6ED4 ED 4B C6 7B          ld      bc, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
351+  6ED8 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
352+  6EDB CD 8D 71             call    neogs_send_dma_block
353+  6EDE DA 64 71             jp      c, .task_dma_error
354+  6EE1                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_task_dma_ok
354+  6EE1             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
354+  6EE1 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
354+  6EE2 21 67 7E    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_task_dma_ok
354+  6EE5 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
354+  6EE8 E1          >                    POP     HL
354+  6EE9             >                ENDIF
355+  6EE9
356+  6EE9                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
357+  6EE9                      ; Stage 5D: execute and fetch framed result.
358+  6EE9                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
359+  6EE9 CD F4 71             call    neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result
360+  6EEC DA 70 71             jp      c, .result_timeout
361+  6EEF
362+  6EEF                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_x86_result_ready
362+  6EEF             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
362+  6EEF E5          >                    PUSH    HL
362+  6EF0 21 9B 7E    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_x86_result_ready
362+  6EF3 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
362+  6EF6 E1          >                    POP     HL
362+  6EF7             >                ENDIF
363+  6EF7 3A CE 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
364+  6EFA 6F                   ld      l, a
365+  6EFB 26 00                ld      h, 0
366+  6EFD                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
366+  6EFD             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
366+  6EFD E5          >                    PUSH    HL
366+  6EFE E5          >                    PUSH    hl
366+  6EFF E1          >                    POP     HL
366+  6F00 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
366+  6F03 E1          >                    POP     HL
366+  6F04             >                ENDIF
367+  6F04                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
367+  6F04             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
367+  6F04 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
367+  6F05 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
367+  6F06 3E 20       >                    LD      A, 32
367+  6F08 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
367+  6F0B ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
367+  6F0D C1          >                    POP     BC
367+  6F0E F1          >                    POP     AF
367+  6F0F             >                ENDIF
368+  6F0F 2A CF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
369+  6F12                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
369+  6F12             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
369+  6F12 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
369+  6F13 E5          >                    PUSH    hl
369+  6F14 E1          >                    POP     HL
369+  6F15 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
369+  6F18 E1          >                    POP     HL
369+  6F19             >                ENDIF
370+  6F19                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
370+  6F19             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
370+  6F19 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
370+  6F1A C5          >                    PUSH    BC
370+  6F1B 3E 0D       >                    LD      A, 13
370+  6F1D 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
370+  6F20 ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
370+  6F22 C1          >                    POP     BC
370+  6F23 F1          >                    POP     AF
370+  6F24             >                ENDIF
371+  6F24
372+  6F24 3A CE 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
373+  6F27 B7                   or      a
374+  6F28 C2 7C 71             jp      nz, .interpreter_error
375+  6F2B
376+  6F2B 2A CF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
377+  6F2E CD 69 8A             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16
378+  6F31 3E 0D                ld      a, 13
379+  6F33 CD 76 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
380+  6F36 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
381+  6F38 CD 76 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
382+  6F3B B7                   or      a
383+  6F3C C3 88 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
384+  6F3F
385+  6F3F              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
386+  6F3F              ; Error exits. All return through the same original-stack restoration.
387+  6F3F              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
388+  6F3F              .diag_dma_error:
389+  6F3F                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
389+  6F3F             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
389+  6F3F E5          >                    PUSH    HL
389+  6F40 21 70 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
389+  6F43 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
389+  6F46 E1          >                    POP     HL
389+  6F47             >                ENDIF
390+  6F47 37                   scf
391+  6F48 C3 88 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
392+  6F4B
393+  6F4B              .diag_protocol_error:
394+  6F4B                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail
394+  6F4B             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
394+  6F4B E5          >                    PUSH    HL
394+  6F4C 21 A5 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail
394+  6F4F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
394+  6F52 E1          >                    POP     HL
394+  6F53             >                ENDIF
395+  6F53 37                   scf
396+  6F54 C3 88 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
397+  6F57
398+  6F57              .diag_execution_error:
399+  6F57                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_status_fail
399+  6F57             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
399+  6F57 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
399+  6F58 21 E4 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_status_fail
399+  6F5B CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
399+  6F5E E1          >                    POP     HL
399+  6F5F             >                ENDIF
400+  6F5F 3A CE 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
401+  6F62 6F                   ld      l, a
402+  6F63 26 00                ld      h, 0
403+  6F65                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
403+  6F65             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
403+  6F65 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
403+  6F66 E5          >                    PUSH    hl
403+  6F67 E1          >                    POP     HL
403+  6F68 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
403+  6F6B E1          >                    POP     HL
403+  6F6C             >                ENDIF
404+  6F6C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_opcode
404+  6F6C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
404+  6F6C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
404+  6F6D 21 1B 7D    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_opcode
404+  6F70 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
404+  6F73 E1          >                    POP     HL
404+  6F74             >                ENDIF
405+  6F74 2A CF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
406+  6F77                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
406+  6F77             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
406+  6F77 E5          >                    PUSH    HL

548+  7170 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
548+  7171 21 0F 80    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_result_timeout
548+  7174 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
548+  7177 E1          >                    POP     HL
548+  7178             >                ENDIF
549+  7178 37                   scf
550+  7179 C3 88 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
551+  717C
552+  717C              .interpreter_error:
553+  717C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_interpreter_status
553+  717C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
553+  717C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
553+  717D 21 40 80    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_interpreter_status
553+  7180 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
553+  7183 E1          >                    POP     HL
553+  7184             >                ENDIF
554+  7184 37                   scf
555+  7185 C3 88 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
556+  7188
557+  7188              .loader_restore_stack:
558+  7188 2A BB 7B             ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
559+  718B F9                   ld      sp, hl
560+  718C C9                   ret
561+  718D
562+  718D              ; =========================================================================
563+  718D              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
564+  718D              ; =========================================================================
565+  718D
566+  718D              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
567+  718D              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
568+  718D              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
569+  718D              ;
570+  718D              ; Protocol:
571+  718D              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
572+  718D              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
573+  718D              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
574+  718D              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
575+  718D              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
576+  718D              neogs_send_dma_block:
577+  718D 78                   ld      a, b
578+  718E B1                   or      c
579+  718F CA E7 71             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
580+  7192 78                   ld      a, b
581+  7193 FE 40                cp      #40
582+  7195 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
583+  7197 C2 E7 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
584+  719A 79                   ld      a, c
585+  719B B7                   or      a
586+  719C C2 E7 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
587+  719F              .dma_length_ok:
588+  719F 22 BD 7B             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
589+  71A2 ED 43 BF 7B          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
590+  71A6
591+  71A6                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
592+  71A6 C5                   push    bc
593+  71A7 E1                   pop     hl
594+  71A8 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
595+  71AB 19                   add     hl, de
596+  71AC 7C                   ld      a, h
597+  71AD FE 30                cp      #30
598+  71AF 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
599+  71B1 C2 E7 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
600+  71B4 7D                   ld      a, l
601+  71B5 B7                   or      a
602+  71B6 C2 E7 71             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
603+  71B9              .dma_total_length_ok:
604+  71B9 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
605+  71BB D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
606+  71BD CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
607+  71C0 D8                   ret     c
608+  71C1
609+  71C1                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
610+  71C1                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
611+  71C1                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
612+  71C1                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
613+  71C1                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
614+  71C1 F3                   di
615+  71C2 2A BF 7B             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
616+  71C5 22 C1 7B             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
617+  71C8 21 C1 7B             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
618+  71CB 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
619+  71CE 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
620+  71D1 ED B0                ldir
621+  71D3
622+  71D3 2A BD 7B             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
623+  71D6 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
624+  71D9 ED 4B BF 7B          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
625+  71DD ED B0                ldir
626+  71DF
627+  71DF                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
628+  71DF 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
629+  71E1 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
630+  71E3 CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
631+  71E6 C9                   ret
632+  71E7
633+  71E7              .dma_bad_length:
634+  71E7 37                   scf
635+  71E8 C9                   ret
636+  71E9
637+  71E9              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
638+  71E9              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
639+  71E9              neogs_run_and_get_result:
640+  71E9 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
641+  71EB D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
642+  71ED CD 0A 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
643+  71F0 D8                   ret     c
644+  71F1 C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
645+  71F4
646+  71F4              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
647+  71F4              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
648+  71F4 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
649+  71F6 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
650+  71F8 CD 0A 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
651+  71FB D8                   ret     c
652+  71FC C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
653+  71FF
654+  71FF              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
655+  71FF              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
656+  71FF              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
657+  71FF 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
658+  7201 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
659+  7203 CD 0A 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
660+  7206 D8                   ret     c
661+  7207 C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
662+  720A
663+  720A              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
664+  720A              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
665+  720A              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
666+  720A              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
667+  720A D5                   push    de
668+  720B 16 10                ld      d, #10
669+  720D              .run_outer:
670+  720D 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
671+  7210              .run_inner:
672+  7210 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
673+  7212 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
674+  7214 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
675+  7216 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
676+  7218 B7                   or      a
677+  7219 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
678+  721B F5                   push    af
679+  721C 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
680+  721F ED 79                out     (c), a
681+  7221 F1                   pop     af
682+  7222 CD 76 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
683+  7225 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
684+  7227
685+  7227              .stream_control:
686+  7227 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
687+  722A 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
688+  722C B7                   or      a
689+  722D 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
690+  722F FE 01                cp      1
691+  7231 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
692+  7233 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
693+  7236 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
694+  7238 32 D4 7B             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
695+  723B CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
696+  723E 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
697+  7240 4F                   ld      c, a
698+  7241 3A D4 7B             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
699+  7244 CD 68 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
700+  7247 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
701+  7249
702+  7249              .stream_clear:
703+  7249 CD 1D 89             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
704+  724C 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
705+  724E
706+  724E              .no_stream_byte:
707+  724E CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
708+  7250 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
709+  7252 2B                   dec     hl
710+  7253 7C                   ld      a, h
711+  7254 B5                   or      l
712+  7255 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
713+  7257 15                   dec     d
714+  7258 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
715+  725A              .run_timeout:
716+  725A D1                   pop     de
717+  725B 37                   scf
718+  725C C9                   ret
719+  725D              .run_done:
720+  725D D1                   pop     de
721+  725E B7                   or      a
722+  725F C9                   ret
723+  7260
724+  7260              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
725+  7260              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
726+  7260 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
727+  7262 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
728+  7264 CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
729+  7267 D8                   ret     c
730+  7268 C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
731+  726B
732+  726B              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
733+  726B              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
734+  726B 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
735+  726D D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
736+  726F CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
737+  7272 D8                   ret     c
738+  7273 C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
739+  7276
740+  7276              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
741+  7276              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
742+  7276 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
743+  7278 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
744+  727A                      ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
745+  727A                      ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
746+  727A CD 0A 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
747+  727D D8                   ret     c
748+  727E C3 B2 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
749+  7281
750+  7281              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
751+  7281              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
752+  7281 3E 05                ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
753+  7283 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
754+  7285                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_sent
754+  7285             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
754+  7285 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
754+  7286 21 19 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_sent
754+  7289 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
754+  728C E1          >                    POP     HL
754+  728D             >                ENDIF
755+  728D CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
756+  7290 30 0A                jr      nc, .memtest_command_ack
757+  7292                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout
757+  7292             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
757+  7292 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
757+  7293 21 8C 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout
757+  7296 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
757+  7299 E1          >                    POP     HL
757+  729A             >                ENDIF
758+  729A 37                   scf
759+  729B C9                   ret
760+  729C              .memtest_command_ack:
761+  729C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_ack
761+  729C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
761+  729C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
761+  729D 21 53 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_ack
761+  72A0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
761+  72A3 E1          >                    POP     HL
761+  72A4             >                ENDIF
762+  72A4 CD B2 72             call    neogs_get_result_frame
763+  72A7 D0                   ret     nc
764+  72A8                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout
764+  72A8             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
764+  72A8 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
764+  72A9 21 D2 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout
764+  72AC CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
764+  72AF E1          >                    POP     HL
764+  72B0             >                ENDIF
765+  72B0 37                   scf
766+  72B1 C9                   ret
767+  72B2
768+  72B2              neogs_get_result_frame:
769+  72B2 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
770+  72B4 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
771+  72B6
772+  72B6 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
773+  72B9 D8                   ret     c
774+  72BA 32 CD 7B             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a
775+  72BD
776+  72BD CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
777+  72C0 D8                   ret     c
778+  72C1 32 CE 7B             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS), a
779+  72C4
780+  72C4 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
781+  72C7 D8                   ret     c
782+  72C8 5F                   ld      e, a
783+  72C9
784+  72C9 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
785+  72CC D8                   ret     c
786+  72CD 57                   ld      d, a
787+  72CE ED 53 CF 7B          ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_AX), de
788+  72D2 ED 53 03 65          ld      (X86_RESULT_AX), de
789+  72D6
790+  72D6 CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
791+  72D9 D8                   ret     c
792+  72DA
793+  72DA 3A CD 7B             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC)
794+  72DD FE A5                cp      NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
795+  72DF 28 02                jr      z, .result_frame_ok
796+  72E1 37                   scf
797+  72E2 C9                   ret
798+  72E3              .result_frame_ok:
799+  72E3 B7                   or      a
800+  72E4 C9                   ret

  1+  8900              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8900              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
  3+  8900              ; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
  4+  8900              ; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
  5+  8900              ; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.

  2+  8B67              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8B67              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8B67              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8B67              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.

  7+  8B67

154+  8C83 3E 0B                ld      a, 11
155+  8C85 4F                   ld      c, a
156+  8C86 3A 90 8D             ld      a, (File_Y)
157+  8C89 57                   ld      d, a
158+  8C8A CD 32 81             call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
159+  8C8D E1                   pop     hl
160+  8C8E
161+  8C8E                      ; Descriptor byte 13 = allocated sector count. Display ceil(sectors/4)
162+  8C8E                      ; without 8-bit overflow: (sectors + 3) is calculated in 16 bits.
163+  8C8E E5                   push    hl
164+  8C8F 11 0D 00             ld      de, 13
165+  8C92 19                   add     hl, de
166+  8C93 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
167+  8C94 5F                   ld      e, a
168+  8C95 16 00                ld      d, 0
169+  8C97 21 03 00             ld      hl, 3
170+  8C9A 19                   add     hl, de
171+  8C9B CB 3C                srl     h
172+  8C9D CB 1D                rr      l
173+  8C9F CB 3C                srl     h
174+  8CA1 CB 1D                rr      l
175+  8CA3 7D                   ld      a, l
176+  8CA4 E1                   pop     hl
177+  8CA5
178+  8CA5 0E 10                ld      c, 16
179+  8CA7 CD 03 8D             call    Util_PrintDecimalByte
180+  8CAA
181+  8CAA 3E 14                ld      a, 20
182+  8CAC 32 8F 8D             ld      (File_X), a
183+  8CAF 3A 90 8D             ld      a, (File_Y)
184+  8CB2 57                   ld      d, a
185+  8CB3 0E 14                ld      c, 20
186+  8CB5 06 4B                ld      b, "K"
187+  8CB7 CD 32 81             call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
188+  8CBA
189+  8CBA 3E 15                ld      a, 21
190+  8CBC 32 8F 8D             ld      (File_X), a
191+  8CBF 3A 90 8D             ld      a, (File_Y)
192+  8CC2 57                   ld      d, a
193+  8CC3 0E 15                ld      c, 21
194+  8CC5 06 62                ld      b, "b"
195+  8CC7 CD 32 81             call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
196+  8CCA
197+  8CCA 3A 90 8D             ld      a, (File_Y)
198+  8CCD 3C                   inc     a
199+  8CCE 32 90 8D             ld      (File_Y), a
200+  8CD1 FE 0B                cp      11
201+  8CD3 D2 F5 8C             jp      nc, .directory_done
202+  8CD6
203+  8CD6                      ; Advance to next 16-byte descriptor. Restore saved slot counter.
204+  8CD6 E1                   pop     hl
205+  8CD7 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
206+  8CDA 19                   add     hl, de
207+  8CDB C1                   pop     bc
208+  8CDC 05                   dec     b
209+  8CDD C2 0E 8C             jp      nz, .loop_slots
210+  8CE0 C3 F7 8C             jp      .directory_success
211+  8CE3
212+  8CE3              .invalid_slot:
213+  8CE3                      ; Drop the per-slot saves, advance HL, and decrement the ORIGINAL
214+  8CE3                      ; 128-entry counter. Do not load 16 into BC (that destroys B), and
215+  8CE3                      ; do not push the per-slot saves again before re-entering the loop.
216+  8CE3 E1                   pop     hl
217+  8CE4 C1                   pop     bc
218+  8CE5 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
219+  8CE8 19                   add     hl, de
220+  8CE9 05                   dec     b
221+  8CEA C2 0E 8C             jp      nz, .loop_slots
222+  8CED C3 F7 8C             jp      .directory_success
223+  8CF0
224+  8CF0              .empty_slot:
225+  8CF0                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
226+  8CF0 E1                   pop     hl
227+  8CF1 C1                   pop     bc
228+  8CF2 C3 F7 8C             jp      .directory_success
229+  8CF5
230+  8CF5              .directory_done:
231+  8CF5                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
232+  8CF5 E1                   pop     hl
233+  8CF6 C1                   pop     bc
234+  8CF7              .directory_success:
235+  8CF7 E1                   pop     hl
236+  8CF8 D1                   pop     de
237+  8CF9 C1                   pop     bc
238+  8CFA F1                   pop     af
239+  8CFB B7                   or      a
240+  8CFC C9                   ret
241+  8CFD
242+  8CFD              .directory_read_error:
243+  8CFD E1                   pop     hl
244+  8CFE D1                   pop     de
245+  8CFF C1                   pop     bc
246+  8D00 F1                   pop     af
247+  8D01 37                   scf
248+  8D02 C9                   ret
249+  8D03
250+  8D03              ; IN: A=value 0..255, C=screen X. Prints exactly three decimal digits.
251+  8D03              Util_PrintDecimalByte:
252+  8D03 F5                   push    af
253+  8D04 C5                   push    bc
254+  8D05 D5                   push    de
255+  8D06 E5                   push    hl
256+  8D07 5F                   ld      e, a
257+  8D08 79                   ld      a, c
258+  8D09 32 8F 8D             ld      (File_X), a
259+  8D0C 7B                   ld      a, e
260+  8D0D 06 00                ld      b, 0
261+  8D0F              .hundreds:
262+  8D0F FE 64                cp      100
263+  8D11 38 05                jr      c, .p_hundreds
264+  8D13 D6 64                sub     100
265+  8D15 04                   inc     b

267+  8D18              .p_hundreds:

269+  8D19 78                   ld      a, b

271+  8D1C D5                   push    de
272+  8D1D 47                   ld      b, a
273+  8D1E 3A 8F 8D             ld      a, (File_X)

277+  8D26 CD 32 81             call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
278+  8D29 3A 8F 8D             ld      a, (File_X)
279+  8D2C 3C                   inc     a
280+  8D2D 32 8F 8D             ld      (File_X), a
281+  8D30 D1                   pop     de
282+  8D31
283+  8D31 7B                   ld      a, e
284+  8D32 06 00                ld      b, 0
285+  8D34              .tens:
286+  8D34 FE 0A                cp      10
287+  8D36 38 05                jr      c, .p_tens
288+  8D38 D6 0A                sub     10
289+  8D3A 04                   inc     b
290+  8D3B 18 F7                jr      .tens
291+  8D3D              .p_tens:

352   F481 ~                    SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
353   F481                      ENDIF
354   F481
355   F481                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 3
